- Base identity: `8409e17` (CS-02 acceptance commit on this branch; main is at 3abf478 and this branch fast-forwards onto it).
- Latest checkpoint: 2026-10-11T08:30:00+00:00; candidate written, review pending. Sources: Unreal 4.26 (the container format the tools accepted; the executable's version string was not read), one IoStore container of 24,107 chunks (Zlib, 11.19 GB uncompressed, 3.40 GB on disk) plus a 64 MB legacy pak with an encrypted index that was not needed. Install cloned to `~/scratch/game-study/roboquest/install/` (5.1 GB); every probe ran on the clone, nothing touched the Steam files or a running process. Pak reader: retoc (Rust, built from source in the study tools folder) lists and converts the container; a hand-written index reader and a hand-written property parser (study tools) read the converted packages. Measured: all 60 data tables (2,823 rows) and 138 curves to the last byte, including enemy stats, the attack timings, weapons, affixes, perks, levels, acts, difficulty and mutators; the 151 generated level pieces and 318 generator packages (footprints from component origins, connector structure, anchor and slot counts); 22,321 packages for asset metadata (1,306 static meshes, 225 skeletal meshes with LOD0 triangles and bones, 210 skeletons, 4,783 clips, 4,190 textures, 1,789 sound waves). Not read: the compiled logic and the blueprint graphs (tile connection rules and spawn counts per anchor stay undecoded). The wiki (roboquest.wiki.gg) gave 66 pages as wikitext plus 17 rendered; four scouts. Frame-counted movement values do not exist: the game was not run, and the movement note states the gap and the capture method.
- Candidate: ten notes and an index under comps/roboquest/, the four-game maps-compared.md completed, borrow rows 27 to 35, forge delta 5; one separable extra, the enemy design grammar with a CC0 and CC-BY base-mesh shortlist (web scout, nothing downloaded), written because Andrew asked whether shipped enemy meshes could be ripped and resculpted; the answer recorded is no (a derivative stays a derivative and would break forge provenance), decision 4 in the pack README.
- Study inputs outside the repository: `~/scratch/game-study/roboquest/` (install clone, wiki wikitext and rendered pages, container listing, converted package subsets, measurement CSVs, table statistics, scout and probe reports). After acceptance the reports, statistics and scripts stay; the converted packages and listings are deleted. **Retention exception, Andrew's decision (11 October 2026):** the install clone is kept as his private modding archive for a game its developers stopped supporting, moved to `~/Projects/roboquest-mod/archive/` with a README (engine, how to list and convert the container, where the tables and generator pieces sit). It sits outside the Scrapkeep study folder, nothing in the Scrapkeep pipeline reads it, and it is never distributed. Recorded here so the cleanup is a deliberate exception, not a forgotten step.
